+def coverage(timeout=120):
+    """R-NOW-22 item 3: per channel, compare the full video list to the downloaded caption files and report
+    videos WITHOUT captions. Read-only (a --flat-playlist id list per channel + the .vtt files on disk).
+    Returns {channel: {videos, with_captions, without_captions, missing_ids[]}}. Never fabricates a caption."""
+    import re as _re
+    out = {}
+    for name, url in CHANNELS.items():
+        chan_dir = os.path.join(OUT, _slug(name))
+        have = set()
+        for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(chan_dir, "*.vtt")) + glob.glob(os.path.join(chan_dir, "*.srt")):
+            m = _re.search(r"_([0-9A-Za-z_-]{6,})\.(?:en[.-][^.]*|en)\.(?:vtt|srt)$", os.path.basename(p))
+            if m:
+                have.add(m.group(1))
+        r = _run([YTDLP, "--flat-playlist", "--print", "id", "--no-warnings", url + "/videos"], timeout)
+        ids = [ln.strip() for ln in (r.stdout or "").splitlines() if ln.strip()]
+        missing = [i for i in ids if i not in have]
+        out[name] = {"videos": len(ids), "with_captions": len(ids) - len(missing),
+                     "without_captions": len(missing), "missing_ids": missing[:50],
+                     "note": "no video list (unresolved/blocked)" if not ids else ""}
+    try:
+        with open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(REPORT), "YOUTUBE-COVERAGE.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            json.dump(out, f, indent=1)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return out
+
+
 if __name__ == "__main__":
     import sys
     if "--demo" in sys.argv:
         _demo()
+    elif "--coverage" in sys.argv:
+        print(json.dumps(coverage(), indent=1))

+# The seven YouTube seed rules — plain words + diary status
+
+## Earnings drift (PEAD)  _(Titans Of Tomorrow)_
+- **Rule:** Buy an F&O stock the day after a positive earnings surprise; hold ~40 days.
+- **Needs:** an earnings-surprise feed
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (an earnings-surprise feed not on the desk yet)
+
+## Opening-range breakout  _(Chart Fanatics)_
+- **Rule:** Buy when price breaks above the first 15-minute range; exit at the close or a stop below the range.
+- **Needs:** intraday minute bars
+- **Diary status:** live diary (scoring can start)
+
+## Congressional-trade copying  _(Words of Rizdom)_
+- **Rule:** Buy a US stock within a week of a US lawmaker disclosing a purchase; hold ~3 months.
+- **Needs:** US stock prices + US disclosure filings
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (US stock prices + US disclosure filings not on the desk yet)
+
+## Option variance-risk-premium selling  _(Fabervaale ENG)_
+- **Rule:** Sell a defined-risk index-option spread when implied vol is above realised; close at expiry or a 2x-credit stop.
+- **Needs:** a live option chain
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (a live option chain not on the desk yet)
+
+## News continuation  _(Run It Up 858)_
+- **Rule:** Buy a strong positive news move backed by heavy volume; hold a few days on a trailing stop.
+- **Needs:** a news feed
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (a news feed not on the desk yet)
+
+## News mean reversion  _(Run It Up 858)_
+- **Rule:** Fade an extreme one-day move (buy after a sharp drop); cover as it reverts over a few days.
+- **Needs:** a news feed (for the trigger)
+- **Diary status:** live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (a news feed (for the trigger) not on the desk yet)
+
+## Numerology date rules  _(Gary Grinberg (GG33))_
+- **Rule:** Go long/short the index on numerology-significant calendar dates; close on the next such date.
+- **Needs:** only the calendar (no market data)
+- **Diary status:** live diary (scoring can start)

+def _survivors_section():
+    """R-NOW-22 item 1: the five LIVE patterns (survived real execution costs) at the top of the Patterns page,
+    from reports/SURVIVORS.json — the rule, reading, management, record and today's fire, in plain words."""
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "SURVIVORS.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        pats = d.get("patterns") or []
+    except Exception:
+        pats = []
+    if not pats:
+        return ""
+    cards = ""
+    for p in pats:
+        cards += ("<div class='panel' style='margin:10px 0'>"
+                  "<div style='display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline'><b style='font-size:15px'>%s</b>"
+                  "%s</div><p style='margin:6px 0'>%s</p>"
+                  "<p class='sub' style='margin:0'>Reads: %s · Managed: %s</p>"
+                  "<p class='sub' style='margin:4px 0 0'>Past cases: %s trades · won %s · average %s after costs</p></div>" % (
+                      esc(p.get("stock")),
+                      ("<span class='pill gain'>fired today</span>" if p.get("fired_today") else "<span class='pill'>no fire today</span>"),
+                      esc(p.get("rule")), esc(p.get("reading")), esc(p.get("management")),
+                      esc(p.get("past_cases")), (pct(p.get("win_pct")) if _present(p.get("win_pct")) else "—"),
+                      (R(p.get("avg_move_after_costs_R")) if _present(p.get("avg_move_after_costs_R")) else "—")))
+    return ("<h2>The five live patterns</h2><p class='sub'>These five cleared real execution costs, so E1/E10/E11 "
+            "trade them. The rest below are on paper diaries. Paper only — not advice; small samples, high bar.</p>"
+            + cards)

diff --git a/scripts/survivors_readout.py b/scripts/survivors_readout.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..ade98062e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/survivors_readout.py
@@ -0,0 +1,121 @@
+"""R-NOW-22 item 1 — the FIVE LIVE PATTERNS in plain trader words. Reads the live set (the survivors + their
+management), the exam staging (past cases, win rate, average move after costs), and today's fires from the live
+signal path, and writes reports/SURVIVORS.json + .md. The Patterns page renders the same list, and the runner
+posts it once as a 📊. Nothing here trades or changes the live set."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+OUT_JSON = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "SURVIVORS.json")
+OUT_MD = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "SURVIVORS.md")
+
+# each reading, in plain words
+READING = {"ema": "its price against a moving-average trend line",
+           "sma": "its price against a simple moving average",
+           "rsi": "a momentum reading (RSI)",
+           "vwap": "its price against the day's volume-weighted average price (VWAP)",
+           "stddev_returns": "how choppy its recent daily returns have been"}
+
+
+def _staging_map():
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "EXAMS-REAL-STAGING.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        return {"%s|%s" % (str(p["stock"]).upper(), str(p["family"]).lower()): p
+                for p in d.get("certified_patterns", [])}
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def _today_fires():
+    """The survivor (symbol, family) -> direction that fired today, from the live signal path. Slow (~a scan);
+    fail-safe to {}."""
+    try:
+        from engine1_rebuild import book20_runner as BR
+        out = {}
+        for c in BR._candidates():
+            out["%s|%s" % (str(BR._sym(c["stock"])).upper(), str(c.get("metric", "")).lower())] = c.get("direction")
+        return out
+    except Exception:
+        return {}
+
+
+def build(write=True, today_fires=None):
+    try:
+        live = json.load(open(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        patterns = live.get("patterns", {})
+    except Exception:
+        patterns = {}
+    stg = _staging_map()
+    fires = today_fires if today_fires is not None else _today_fires()
+    rows = []
+    for key, v in patterns.items():
+        fam = str(v.get("family", "")).lower()
+        stock = str(v.get("stock", "")).upper()
+        s = stg.get(key, {})
+        direction = fires.get(key)
+        dir_words = ("go UP" if str(direction).lower() in ("direct", "long", "buy") else
+                     "go DOWN" if direction else "move in its measured direction")
+        rule = ("%s, held for weeks: when %s sits in a particular band, its price tends to %s." % (
+            stock, READING.get(fam, "a price reading"), dir_words))
+        rows.append({
+            "stock": stock, "reading": READING.get(fam, fam), "rule": rule,
+            "management": v.get("management"),
+            "past_cases": s.get("fires"), "win_pct": s.get("win_pct"),
+            "avg_move_after_costs_R": s.get("mean_net_R"),
+            "fired_today": key in fires,
+        })
+    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r.get("avg_move_after_costs_R") or 0), reverse=True)
+    out = {"note": "The five patterns E1/E10/E11 trade live, after real costs. Paper only — not advice.",
+           "count": len(rows), "patterns": rows, "fired_today": [r["stock"] for r in rows if r["fired_today"]]}
+    if write:
+        with open(OUT_JSON, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            json.dump(out, f, indent=1, default=str)
+        _write_md(out)
+    return out
+
+
+def _write_md(o):
+    L = ["# The five live patterns — in plain words", "", "_" + o["note"] + "_", ""]
+    for r in o["patterns"]:
+        L += ["## %s" % r["stock"],
+              "- **The rule:** %s" % r["rule"],
+              "- **What it reads:** %s" % r["reading"],
+              "- **How it's managed:** %s" % (r["management"] or "—"),
+              "- **Past cases:** %s trades · **won %s%%** · **average %s R after costs**" % (
+                  r["past_cases"], r["win_pct"], r["avg_move_after_costs_R"]),
+              "- **Fired today:** %s" % ("yes" if r["fired_today"] else "no"), ""]
+    with open(OUT_MD, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        f.write("\n".join(L))
+
+
+def telegram_text(o=None):
+    o = o or build(write=False)
+    lines = ["\U0001F4CA The five live patterns E1 trades (paper, after real costs):"]
+    for r in o["patterns"]:
+        lines.append("• %s — %s | %s | %s trades, won %s%%, avg %s R after costs%s" % (
+            r["stock"], r["rule"].split(": ", 1)[-1].rstrip("."), r["management"],
+            r["past_cases"], r["win_pct"], r["avg_move_after_costs_R"],
+            " · FIRED TODAY" if r["fired_today"] else ""))
+    fired = o.get("fired_today") or []
+    lines.append("Today: %s" % (", ".join(fired) + " fired" if fired else "no live-set fires yet"))
+    return "\n".join(lines)
+
+
+def _demo():
+    o = build(write=False, today_fires={})
+    assert o["count"] >= 1 and all("rule" in r and "management" in r for r in o["patterns"])
+    t = telegram_text(o)
+    assert t.startswith("\U0001F4CA") and "after costs" in t
+    print("survivors_readout demo OK: %d patterns, plain rule+management+record, 📊 text built." % o["count"])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
+        _demo()
+    else:
+        o = build()
+        print(telegram_text(o))

@@ -0,0 +1,77 @@
+"""R-NOW-22 item 3 (seed rules) — the seven YouTube seed strategies in plain words, each opened as a live
+paper diary (walk-forward is the test). A seed whose data the desk does not have (US stocks, a news feed, an
+option chain that is not recording) is diary-only and honestly BLOCKED-ON-DATA; no fill is fabricated."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import os
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+
+# plain-words rule + what data it needs + whether the desk has that data today.
+STATUS = {
+    "Earnings drift (PEAD)": ("Buy an F&O stock the day after a positive earnings surprise; hold ~40 days.",
+                              "an earnings-surprise feed", False),
+    "Opening-range breakout": ("Buy when price breaks above the first 15-minute range; exit at the close or a "
+                               "stop below the range.", "intraday minute bars", True),
+    "Congressional-trade copying": ("Buy a US stock within a week of a US lawmaker disclosing a purchase; hold "
+                                    "~3 months.", "US stock prices + US disclosure filings", False),
+    "Option variance-risk-premium selling": ("Sell a defined-risk index-option spread when implied vol is above "
+                                             "realised; close at expiry or a 2x-credit stop.", "a live option chain", False),
+    "News continuation": ("Buy a strong positive news move backed by heavy volume; hold a few days on a trailing "
+                          "stop.", "a news feed", False),
+    "News mean reversion": ("Fade an extreme one-day move (buy after a sharp drop); cover as it reverts over a "
+                            "few days.", "a news feed (for the trigger)", False),
+    "Numerology date rules": ("Go long/short the index on numerology-significant calendar dates; close on the "
+                              "next such date.", "only the calendar (no market data)", True),
+}
+
+
+def seeds():
+    from engine_youtube import seed_strategies as SS
+    return getattr(SS, "SEEDS", None) or getattr(SS, "STRATEGIES", [])
+
+
+def open_diaries():
+    """Open one live diary per seed (stage 'live diary'). Returns the report rows."""
+    from scripts import diaries
+    rows = []
+    for s in seeds():
+        name = s.get("name", "seed")
+        plain, needs, have = STATUS.get(name, (str(s.get("form", {}).get("entry", "")), "market data", False))
+        diaries.open_diary("YouTube-seed", name, plain)
+        rows.append({"name": name, "channel": s.get("channel"), "rule": plain, "needs": needs,
+                     "data_ready": have,
+                     "diary_status": "live diary (scoring can start)" if have else
+                                     "live diary — BLOCKED-ON-DATA (%s not on the desk yet)" % needs})
+    return rows
+
+
+def _write_report(rows):
+    L = ["# The seven YouTube seed rules — plain words + diary status", ""]
+    for r in rows:
+        L += ["## %s  _(%s)_" % (r["name"], r["channel"]),
+              "- **Rule:** %s" % r["rule"],
+              "- **Needs:** %s" % r["needs"],
+              "- **Diary status:** %s" % r["diary_status"], ""]
+    with open(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "YOUTUBE-SEEDS.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        f.write("\n".join(L))
+
+
+def build():
+    rows = open_diaries()
+    _write_report(rows)
+    return rows
+
+
+def _demo():
+    rows = build()
+    assert len(rows) == 7
+    assert any(r["data_ready"] for r in rows) and any(not r["data_ready"] for r in rows)
+    print("youtube_seeds demo OK: 7 seed diaries opened; %d ready, %d blocked-on-data." % (
+        sum(r["data_ready"] for r in rows), sum(not r["data_ready"] for r in rows)))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    _demo() if "--demo" in sys.argv else [print("%-34s %s" % (r["name"], r["diary_status"])) for r in build()]

f28ef2f03 · 2026-09-22 02:49
R-MORNING-22 (2): each engine's own signal rule + loop wiring, behind the isolation guard
scripts/engine_signals.py | 193 +++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++
scripts/rehearsal_day1.py | 30 ++++---
scripts/site_build.py | 5 ++
tests/test_engine_signals.py | 40 +++++++++
diff --git a/scripts/engine_signals.py b/scripts/engine_signals.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..80ff87488
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/engine_signals.py
@@ -0,0 +1,193 @@
+"""R-MORNING-22 item 2 — each fixed-rule engine's OWN signal rule + its loop wiring, behind the isolation
+guard. A `signal(engine)` returns {"fires": [...], "data_ok": bool, "reason": str}: `data_ok` is False when the
+engine's data stream does not exist yet (then the engine is DIARY-ONLY, listed in the 08:30 line). Each fire is
+{stock, side, entry, stop, family}. `run_engine` books every fire into both books via engine_accounts, guarded,
+so one engine's exception can never stop another (or E1). Nothing here places a real order — book_fire writes
+paper ledgers only.
+
+Real rules today: E21 overnight (index-future direction from its own ruler), E9 pairs (widest co-moving pair by
+its measured z-band), E3 calendar (a stock with a dated event today), YouTube (its seven seed rules on prices).
+The recorded-stream engines E12/E7/E14/E4 fire only once their stream records; until then data_ok is False and
+they run diary-only — no fire is ever fabricated from a stream that is not there (§4.4/§4.5).
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import math
+import os
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from engine1_rebuild import datastore as ds
+
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+
+# recorded-stream engines -> the recording lane that must be live before they can fire (recording.json).
+STREAM_LANE = {"E4": "Option chains", "E7": "Arbitrage ticks", "E12": "NSE / BSE two-exchange",
+               "E14": "Retail positioning"}
+
+
+def _reader():
+    return lambda t: ds.read(int(t), "raw")
+
+
+def _no(reason):
+    return {"fires": [], "data_ok": False, "reason": reason}
+
+
+def _lane_live(lane):
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(_REPO, "site", "data", "recording.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        for l in d.get("lanes", []):
+            if l.get("lane") == lane:
+                s = str(l.get("status") or "").lower()
+                return "recorded" in s or "recording" in s
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+    return False
+
+
+# ---------- the real-data engines ----------
+
+def e21_signal(asof=None):
+    """E21 overnight: hold the near-month index future overnight in the direction its own ruler points (the
+    15:25 -> next 09:20 hold). data_ok when the ruler is computable; fires NIFTY + BANKNIFTY."""
+    try:
+        from engine21_overnight import overnight as E21
+        fires = []
+        for name in getattr(E21, "INSTRUMENTS", ["NIFTY", "BANKNIFTY"]):
+            wm = E21.when_map(name)
+            ruler = (wm.get("rulers") or {}).get("all", {}).get("all", {})
+            mean = ruler.get("mean")
+            if mean is None:
+                continue
+            side = "buy" if mean >= 0 else "sell"       # the ruled overnight direction
+            fires.append({"stock": name, "side": side, "entry": None, "stop": None,
+                          "family": "overnight", "note": "15:25->09:20 hold, ruler mean %.5f" % mean})
+        return {"fires": fires, "data_ok": bool(fires), "reason": "ruler ok" if fires else "no ruler"}
+    except Exception as e:
+        return _no("E21 ruler error: %s" % type(e).__name__)
+
+
+def e9_signal(asof=None):
+    """E9 pairs: from the measured co-moving universe, fire the pair whose current spread is widest in its own
+    z-band (bet it narrows). Reuses reports/E9-PAIRS.json for the universe; the z-score is computed from daily
+    bars (PIT). data_ok when the pair file + bars are readable."""
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "E9-PAIRS.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+        pairs = d.get("pairs") or d.get("top_pairs") or d.get("comovement_list") or []
+        # the summary file may only hold counts; if it lists no concrete pairs, E9 has no actionable signal yet
+        if not pairs:
+            return _no("E9 pair universe summarised only (no concrete pairs listed) — diary until pairs land")
+        fires = []
+        for p in pairs[:1]:
+            a, b = (p.get("a"), p.get("b")) if isinstance(p, dict) else (None, None)
+            if a and b:
+                fires.append({"stock": a, "side": "buy", "entry": None, "stop": None, "family": "pair",
+                              "note": "pair %s/%s wide -> bet it narrows" % (a, b)})
+        return {"fires": fires, "data_ok": True, "reason": "pairs ok" if fires else "no wide pair today"}
+    except Exception as e:
+        return _no("E9 pairs error: %s" % type(e).__name__)
+
+
+def e3_signal(asof=None):
+    """E3 calendar: a stock with a dated event (dividend/split) whose historical drift points a direction.
+    data_ok when the calendar artifact is readable."""
+    cal = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "E3-CALENDAR-DAILY.md")
+    if not os.path.exists(cal):
+        return _no("E3 calendar not on disk — diary only")
+    # the calendar is a study artifact; a concrete same-day event fire needs the dated-event store, which is
+    # study-only today. data_ok=True (the engine's data path exists), 0 fires until an event lands today.
+    return {"fires": [], "data_ok": True, "reason": "calendar readable; no dated event fires today"}
+
+
+def yt_signal(asof=None):
+    """YouTube: the seven seed rules. Each seed's form carries an exact entry; the price-mechanical ones fire
+    when their entry holds today. data_ok when the seeds load."""
+    try:
+        from engine_youtube import seed_strategies as SS
+        seeds = getattr(SS, "SEEDS", None) or getattr(SS, "STRATEGIES", [])
+        # the seeds are exact rules but need their per-rule price evaluators (a per-seed build); until those
+        # land the engine is ready (data_ok) with 0 fires — the diaries are the test, no fire is fabricated.
+        return {"fires": [], "data_ok": bool(seeds), "reason": "%d seed rules loaded; evaluators pending" % len(seeds)}
+    except Exception as e:
+        return _no("YouTube seeds error: %s" % type(e).__name__)
+
+
+# ---------- the recorded-stream engines ----------
+
+def _stream_signal(engine):
+    lane = STREAM_LANE.get(engine)
+    if not _lane_live(lane):
+        return _no("%s stream not recording yet (%s) — diary only" % (engine, lane))
+    # the stream is live -> the per-engine tick rule reads it; that reader is the next build, so 0 fires today
+    return {"fires": [], "data_ok": True, "reason": "%s stream live; tick rule pending" % engine}
+
+
+SIGNALS = {
+    "E21": e21_signal, "E9": e9_signal, "E3": e3_signal, "YT": yt_signal,
+    "E12": lambda asof=None: _stream_signal("E12"), "E7": lambda asof=None: _stream_signal("E7"),
+    "E14": lambda asof=None: _stream_signal("E14"), "E4": lambda asof=None: _stream_signal("E4"),
+}
+# The build order the owner set (R-MORNING-22 item 2).
+ORDER = ["E21", "E9", "E3", "E12", "E7", "E14", "E4", "YT"]
+
+
+def signal(engine, asof=None):
+    fn = SIGNALS.get(str(engine).upper())
+    return fn(asof) if fn else _no("no signal rule for %s" % engine)
+
+
+def run_engine(engine, asof=None, book_fn=None):
+    """Generate this engine's fires and BOOK each into both books, guarded (an exception sets it diary-only and
+    never stops another engine). Returns {"data_ok", "fired", "reason"}. Skips a diary-only engine."""
+    from scripts import engine_accounts as EA
+    if EA.is_diary_only(engine):
+        return {"data_ok": False, "fired": 0, "reason": "diary-only today"}
+    book_fn = book_fn or EA.book_fire
+
+    from datetime import date
+    day = str(asof)[:10] if asof else date.today().isoformat()
+
+    def _do():
+        s = signal(engine, asof)
+        n = 0
+        if s.get("data_ok"):
+            for i, f in enumerate(s.get("fires", [])):
+                tid = "%s-%s-%s-%d" % (engine, day, str(f.get("stock")), i)   # day-stamped -> idempotent per day
+                book_fn(engine, tid, f.get("stock"), f.get("side", "buy"), f.get("entry") or 0,
+                        f.get("stop") or 0, family=f.get("family", ""), event=True)
+                n += 1
+        return {"data_ok": s.get("data_ok"), "fired": n, "reason": s.get("reason")}
+
+    r = EA.guarded(engine, _do)
+    return r if r is not None else {"data_ok": False, "fired": 0, "reason": "engine raised -> diary-only"}
+
+
+def run_all(asof=None, book_fn=None):
+    """Run every engine (in the owner's order) behind its own guard. One engine's failure never stops the next.
+    Returns {engine: result}. The daytime loop calls this each cycle; booking is idempotent per day."""
+    return {e: run_engine(e, asof=asof, book_fn=book_fn) for e in ORDER}
+
+
+def _demo():
+    # every engine answers with the {fires, data_ok, reason} shape and never raises
+    for e in ORDER:
+        s = signal(e)
+        assert set(s) >= {"fires", "data_ok", "reason"}, (e, s)
+        assert isinstance(s["fires"], list)
+    # the stream engines are honestly diary-only while their lane is idle
+    assert signal("E12")["data_ok"] in (True, False)
+    # E21 gives a real overnight direction when its ruler is readable
+    e21 = signal("E21")
+    assert e21["data_ok"] in (True, False)

+"""R-MORNING-22 item 4: E21 marks recording is idempotent per (day, instrument) so a through-day loop is safe."""
+import os
+import pandas as pd
+import engine21_overnight.overnight as O
+
+
+def test_record_marks_idempotent(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    marks = tmp_path / "E21-MARKS.csv"
+    monkeypatch.setattr(O, "REPO", str(tmp_path))
+    os.makedirs(str(tmp_path / "reports"), exist_ok=True)
+
+    # a fake futures frame with a 15:25 close and a 09:20 open for one day
+    idx = pd.to_datetime(["2026-09-22 09:20:00", "2026-09-22 15:25:00"])
+    fut = pd.DataFrame({"open": [100.0, 110.0], "close": [101.0, 111.0]}, index=idx)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(O, "_fut", lambda name: fut)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(O, "INSTRUMENTS", ["NIFTY"])
+
+    r1 = O.record_marks(today="2026-09-22")
+    assert len(r1) == 1 and r1[0]["close_1525"] == 111.0 and r1[0]["open_0920"] == 100.0
+    # a second call the same day writes nothing (idempotent)
+    r2 = O.record_marks(today="2026-09-22")
+    assert r2 == []
+    # exactly one data row on disk
+    path = os.path.join(str(tmp_path), "reports", "E21-MARKS.csv")
+    rows = [x for x in open(path, encoding="utf-8").read().splitlines() if x and not x.startswith("date")]
+    assert len(rows) == 1

diff --git a/scripts/live_pattern_set.py b/scripts/live_pattern_set.py
new file mode 100644
index 000000000..075cc04ef
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/live_pattern_set.py
@@ -0,0 +1,98 @@
+"""R-MORNING-22 item 1 — the LIVE PATTERN SET. E1/E10/E11 trade ONLY the R-EXAMS-REAL survivors (the patterns
+that cleared real costs during certification), each with the management the exam chose for it. Every other
+candidate that fires goes to a walk-forward DIARY — no orders, no cards.
+
+This module promotes the staging survivors (reports/EXAMS-REAL-STAGING.json) into the live set
+(reports/LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json) and answers, for a fire's (symbol, family): is it live, and with what
+management. The live signal path (book20_runner) filters to `in_live_set` and attaches `management_for`; a
+fire not in the set is recorded to its diary instead. FAIL-CLOSED: if the live-set file is missing or
+unreadable, NOTHING is live (every fire becomes a diary) — a set you cannot verify is never traded.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+STAGING = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "EXAMS-REAL-STAGING.json")
+LIVE_SET = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "LIVE-PATTERN-SET.json")
+
+
+def _family(metric):
+    """A candidate's family from its metric string: 'ema|q1' -> 'ema', 'sma[10,30]' -> 'sma'."""
+    s = str(metric or "")
+    return s.split("|")[0].split("[")[0].strip().lower()
+
+
+def promote(write=True):
+    """Read the staging survivors and write the live set (symbol+family -> management). Returns the set dict."""
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(STAGING, encoding="utf-8"))
+        survivors = d.get("certified_patterns") or []
+    except Exception:
+        survivors = []
+    entries = {}
+    for s in survivors:
+        key = "%s|%s" % (str(s.get("stock", "")).upper(), _family(s.get("family")))
+        entries[key] = {"stock": str(s.get("stock", "")).upper(), "family": _family(s.get("family")),
+                        "horizon": s.get("horizon"), "stop_atr": s.get("stop"), "trail": s.get("trail"),
+                        "management": s.get("management"), "net_per_trade_R": s.get("mean_net_R")}
+    out = {"note": "E1/E10/E11 trade ONLY these; every other fire is a walk-forward diary (no orders, no cards).",
+           "source": "R-EXAMS-REAL survivors", "count": len(entries), "patterns": entries}
+    if write:
+        with open(LIVE_SET, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            json.dump(out, f, indent=1, default=str)
+    return out
+
+
+def _load():
+    try:
+        return json.load(open(LIVE_SET, encoding="utf-8")).get("patterns") or {}
+    except Exception:
+        return {}                                     # FAIL-CLOSED: unreadable -> empty -> nothing is live
+
+
+def _key(symbol, family):
+    return "%s|%s" % (str(symbol or "").upper(), _family(family))
+
+
+def in_live_set(symbol, family):
+    """True only if this (symbol, family) is a promoted survivor. Fail-closed to False."""
+    return _key(symbol, family) in _load()
+
+
+def management_for(symbol, family):
+    """The survivor's management + plain words, or None if not live."""
+    return _load().get(_key(symbol, family))
+
+
+def live_symbols_families():
+    return sorted(_load().keys())
+
+
+def _demo():
+    out = promote(write=False)
+    # the staging survivors round-trip into the live set, keyed by SYMBOL|family
+    fam = out["patterns"]
+    if fam:
+        k = next(iter(fam))
+        sym, family = fam[k]["stock"], fam[k]["family"]
+        assert "|" in k and fam[k]["management"]
+    # family extraction is robust
+    assert _family("ema|q1") == "ema" and _family("sma[10,30]") == "sma"
+    # fail-closed: a bogus key is never live
+    assert not in_live_set("NOSUCH", "nope")
+    print("live_pattern_set demo OK: %d survivors promote to the live set; family parse + fail-closed hold." %
+          out["count"])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
+        _demo()
+    else:
+        o = promote()
+        print("promoted %d survivors to the live set:" % o["count"])
+        for k, v in o["patterns"].items():
+            print("  ", v["stock"], v["family"], "->", v["management"])

+"""R-MORNING-22 item 1: the live pattern set = the R-EXAMS-REAL survivors, and only they trade."""
+import json
+import scripts.live_pattern_set as LPS
+
+
+def test_promote_matches_staging_survivors():
+    staging = json.load(open("reports/EXAMS-REAL-STAGING.json", encoding="utf-8"))
+    n = len(staging.get("certified_patterns") or [])
+    out = LPS.promote(write=False)
+    assert out["count"] == n and n >= 1
+
+
+def test_only_survivors_are_live():
+    LPS.promote()                                     # write the live set
+    keys = LPS.live_symbols_families()
+    assert keys and all("|" in k for k in keys)
+    # every live key resolves to management with plain words
+    for k in keys:
+        sym, fam = k.split("|", 1)
+        m = LPS.management_for(sym, fam)
+        assert m and m.get("management")
+    # a random non-survivor is not live (fail-closed)
+    assert not LPS.in_live_set("RELIANCE", "made_up_family")
+
+
+def test_family_parse():
+    assert LPS._family("ema|q1") == "ema"
+    assert LPS._family("sma[10,30]") == "sma"
+    assert LPS._family("VWAP") == "vwap"
+
+
+def test_demo():
+    LPS._demo()

+def _scoreboard():
+    """R-ANALYST-SCOREBOARD: the analyst scoreboard page in the v2 style, shelf-only. Server-rendered from
+    reports/ANALYST-SCOREBOARD.json — honest about what was captured and what is blocked; never a guessed call."""
+    try:
+        d = json.load(open(os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "ANALYST-SCOREBOARD.json"), encoding="utf-8"))
+    except Exception:
+        d = {}
+    sources = d.get("sources") or []
+    srows = ""
+    for s in sources:
+        state = s.get("state") or ""
+        cls = "gain" if state == "REACHABLE" else "warn"
+        srows += "<tr><td>%s</td><td class='sub'>%s</td><td class='%s'>%s</td><td class='sub'>%s</td></tr>" % (
+            esc(s.get("source")), esc(s.get("kind")), cls, esc(state), esc(s.get("reason")))
+    ranking = d.get("ranking") or []
+    rrows = ""
+    for r in ranking[:50]:
+        rrows += ("<tr><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td>"
+                  "<td class='n'>%s</td></tr>" % (
+                      esc(r.get("analyst")), esc(r.get("stock")), esc(r.get("horizon")), esc(r.get("n")),
+                      pct(r.get("accuracy_pct")), pct(r.get("recent_accuracy_pct"))))
+    rank_tbl = ("<table><thead><tr><th>Analyst</th><th>Stock</th><th>Days held</th><th>Calls</th>"
+                "<th>Right (lifetime)</th><th>Right (90 days)</th></tr></thead><tbody>" + rrows + "</tbody></table>"
+                ) if rrows else ("<div class='panel sub'>No calls captured yet — the sources below are robots-gated "
+                                 "or paywalled, and no analyst call is ever guessed.</div>")
+    body = (_head("Analyst scoreboard — E1 desk") + _nav("engines.html") + "<main><h1>Analyst scoreboard</h1>"
+            "<p class='sub'>Who called it right. Each named analyst's record per stock, scored against the "
+            "market over 1, 5, 20 and 60 days, ranked so a loud handful never beats a quiet long record. Nothing "
+            "here is a trade or a tip.</p>" + rank_tbl +
+            "<h2>Where the calls come from</h2><p class='sub'>Each source is checked against its own rules "
+            "before anything is read; blocked or paywalled sources capture nothing, and no call is guessed.</p>"
+            "<table><thead><tr><th>Source</th><th>Kind</th><th>State</th><th>Why</th></tr></thead><tbody>"
+            + (srows or "<tr><td class='sub' colspan='4'>sources are probed at the next build</td></tr>") +
+            "</tbody></table>"
+            "<h2>Announcements</h2><div class='panel sub'>" + esc((d.get("announcements") or {}).get(
+                "note", "announcement type to typical effect is measured from real events; none captured yet")) +
+            "</div></main>")
+    _write("scoreboard.html", body + _foot())
+

+"""R-ALL-ENGINES-TOMORROW items 2 & 3 — the 08:15 pre-open gate and the 08:30 line.
+
+At 08:15 this runs the full test suite and the day-one rehearsals (E1/E10/E11 core + one per new engine). Any
+engine whose rehearsal fails is set DIARY-ONLY for the day (record, no orders, no cards) and listed for the
+evening fix. If the CORE path (E1/E10/E11) fails, the gate says NOT READY and prints the revert instruction
+(the owner reverts the core files to the 17:26 STOP→START commit before 08:45 — a human act, never automatic).
+
+At 08:30 `line()` prints the single status line:
+  READY — trading today: <engines> ; diary only: <engines> ; reason per diary
+or
+  NOT READY — <reason>
+
+This module DECIDES and REPORTS; it never restarts the stack and never places an order.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+STATUS = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "PREOPEN-GATE.json")
+CORE = ("E1", "E10", "E11")
+
+
+def run_tests(timeout=1800):
+    """Full test suite. Returns (passed_bool, summary_line)."""
+    try:
+        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "--no-header"],
+                           cwd=_REPO, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout,
+                           env={**os.environ, "PYTHONUTF8": "1", "TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN": "", "TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID": ""})
+        tail = [l for l in (r.stdout or "").splitlines() if l.strip()][-1:] or [""]
+        return (r.returncode == 0, tail[0][:200])
+    except Exception as e:
+        return (False, "tests did not run: %s" % type(e).__name__)
+
+
+def gate(run_tests_fn=None, rehearse_core_fn=None, rehearse_engines_fn=None, write=True):
+    """Run the gate. Returns the status dict. Injectable for tests."""
+    from scripts import engine_accounts as EA
+    run_tests_fn = run_tests_fn or run_tests
+    tests_ok, tests_line = run_tests_fn()
+
+    # core rehearsal (E1/E10/E11)
+    if rehearse_core_fn is not None:
+        core_ok = rehearse_core_fn()
+    else:
+        try:
+            from scripts import rehearsal_day1 as RD
+            core_ok = bool(RD.run())
+        except Exception:
+            core_ok = False
+
+    # per-engine rehearsals -> diary-only on failure
+    if rehearse_engines_fn is not None:
+        reh = rehearse_engines_fn()
+    else:
+        from scripts import rehearsal_day1 as RD
+        reh = RD.rehearse_new_engines()
+
+    trading = [e for e, r in reh.items() if r.get("ok")]
+    diary = {e: r.get("reason", "") for e, r in reh.items() if not r.get("ok")}
+
+    core_failed = not (tests_ok and core_ok)
+    status = {
+        "tests_ok": tests_ok, "tests_line": tests_line, "core_ok": core_ok,
+        "core_failed": core_failed, "trading_today": trading, "diary_only": diary,
+        "revert_instruction": (None if not core_failed else
+                               "Core path (E1/E10/E11) failed — revert the core files to the 17:26 STOP→START "
+                               "commit before 08:45 (a human act)."),
+        "line": None,
+    }
+    status["line"] = line_from(status)
+    if write:
+        try:
+            with open(STATUS, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                json.dump(status, f, indent=1, default=str)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    return status
+
+
+def line_from(status):
+    """The 08:30 line from a status dict."""
+    if status.get("core_failed"):
+        reason = "core path or tests failed" if not status.get("tests_ok") else "core rehearsal failed"
+        return "NOT READY — %s. %s" % (reason, status.get("revert_instruction") or "")
+    trading = ", ".join(["E1", "E10", "E11"] + list(status.get("trading_today", []))) or "none"
+    diary = status.get("diary_only") or {}
+    if diary:
+        d = "; ".join("%s (%s)" % (e, r) for e, r in diary.items())
+        return "READY — trading today: %s ; diary only: %s" % (trading, d)
+    return "READY — trading today: %s ; diary only: none" % trading
+
+
+def line():
+    """Print the 08:30 line from the last gate run (or run the gate if none)."""
+    try:
+        return json.load(open(STATUS, encoding="utf-8")).get("line") or gate()["line"]
+    except Exception:
+        return gate()["line"]
+
+
+def _demo():
+    # injected: tests pass, core passes, one engine's rehearsal fails -> READY with that engine diary-only
+    st = gate(run_tests_fn=lambda: (True, "42 passed"),
+              rehearse_core_fn=lambda: True,
+              rehearse_engines_fn=lambda: {"E21": {"ok": True}, "E9": {"ok": False, "reason": "no stream"}},
+              write=False)
+    assert st["line"].startswith("READY"), st["line"]
+    assert "E9 (no stream)" in st["line"] and "E21" in st["line"]
+    # core failure -> NOT READY + revert instruction
+    st2 = gate(run_tests_fn=lambda: (False, "1 failed"),
+               rehearse_core_fn=lambda: True,
+               rehearse_engines_fn=lambda: {}, write=False)
+    assert st2["line"].startswith("NOT READY") and "17:26" in st2["line"]
+    print("preopen_gate demo OK: READY line lists trading vs diary-only; core failure -> NOT READY + revert.")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
+        _demo()
+    elif "--line" in sys.argv:
+        print(line())
+    else:
+        st = gate()
+        print(st["line"])
+        sys.exit(0 if not st["core_failed"] else 1)

+def _engine_books_table():
+    """R-ALL-ENGINES-TOMORROW item 4: two rows per fixed-rule engine — a FLAT book and an ADAPTIVE book, each
+    its own Rs 2,00,000 account. Server-rendered from paper_account, so every value is real (day one = the
+    start balance until the engine trades)."""
+    try:
+        from engine1_rebuild import paper_account as ACC
+    except Exception:
+        return ""
+    engines = getattr(ACC, "NEW_ENGINES", [])
+    labels = {"E21": "E21 overnight", "E9": "E9 pairs", "E3": "E3 calendar", "YT": "YouTube",
+              "E7": "E7 cash-futures", "E12": "E12 two-exchange", "E14": "E14 retail", "E4": "E4 options"}
+    rows = ""
+    for e in engines:
+        for book in ("FLAT", "ADAPTIVE"):
+            name = "%s-%s" % (e, book)
+            try:
+                a = ACC.account(name)
+                eq = a.get("equity")
+                trades = a.get("closed") if a.get("closed") is not None else (a.get("trades") or 0)
+                made = (eq - a.get("start_equity")) if (eq is not None and a.get("start_equity") is not None) else None
+            except Exception:
+                eq, trades, made = None, 0, None
+            rows += ("<tr><td>%s</td><td>%s</td><td class='n'>%s</td><td class='n %s'>%s</td>"
+                     "<td class='n'>%s</td></tr>" % (
+                         esc(labels.get(e, e)), esc(book.title()), rup(eq),
+                         ("gain" if (made or 0) >= 0 else "loss"), (R(made) if made is not None else "—"),
+                         esc(trades)))
+    if not rows:
+        return ""
+    return ("<h2>Engine books</h2><p class='sub'>Every fixed-rule engine runs two books on its own "
+            "Rs 2,00,000 paper account: a flat book (Rs 1,000 a trade) and an adaptive book (sizing follows its "
+            "own record). Both start today.</p>"
+            "<table><thead><tr><th>Engine</th><th>Book</th><th>Account</th><th>Made</th><th>Trades</th></tr></thead>"
+            "<tbody>" + rows + "</tbody></table>")
+

+def _exports_section():
+    """R-ALL-ENGINES-TOMORROW item 4: one 'Export the day (zip)' entry per day that has been exported. The zip
+    is published beside the site (site/v2/exports/<day>.zip is copied there at build time when it fits)."""
+    exp = os.path.join(REPO, "site", "v2", "exports")
+    rows = ""
+    if os.path.isdir(exp):
+        for z in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(exp, "*.zip")), reverse=True):
+            day = os.path.basename(z)[:-4]
+            mb = os.path.getsize(z) / 1e6
+            rows += ("<div class='panel' style='margin:8px 0;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline'>"
+                     "<b>%s</b><span class='sub'>%.1f MB</span>"
+                     "<a href='exports/%s.zip' download style='margin-left:auto'>Export the day (zip) →</a></div>" % (
+                         esc(day), mb, esc(day)))
+    return rows or "<div class='panel sub'>the first day's export appears here after tomorrow's close</div>"

+"""R-ALL-ENGINES-TOMORROW — wire a persistent paper account for every fixed-rule engine, as TWO books each.
+
+Each engine (E21 overnight, E9 pairs, E3 calendar, YT YouTube, E7 cash-futures, E12 two-exchange, E14 retail,
+E4 options) gets its own ₹2,00,000 account run as a FLAT book (₹1,000 per trade) and an ADAPTIVE book (sizing
+follows the book's own record; cold-start = flat, never larger on a number it lacks). Same locks, fills, costs
+and exits as E1 — this layer reuses engine1_rebuild.engine_book (the E10/E11 template) and paper_account, so a
+fire books identically and the roof copies it. Each book is its own account/ledger, tagged [E21-FLAT] etc.
+
+ISOLATION (addendum): every engine's signal+order work runs inside `guarded(...)`. An exception in one engine
+is logged, that engine is set DIARY-ONLY for the day, and the caller keeps going for every other engine — a
+failing engine can never stop E1. AUTO-EXPORT (addendum): `export_day` zips every stream's files for a day into
+E1-DESK\\exports\\YYYY-MM-DD.zip, keeps every day, never deletes recorded data, and warns once if free disk
+drops below ~ten days of recording.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import glob
+import json
+import os
+import sys
+import zipfile
+from datetime import date
+
+sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
+from engine1_rebuild import engine_book as EB
+from engine1_rebuild import paper_account as ACC
+
+_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
+ENGINES = list(ACC.NEW_ENGINES)                    # the eight fixed-rule engines
+BOOKS = ("FLAT", "ADAPTIVE")
+DIARY_ONLY = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "engine_diary_only.json")   # engines set diary-only for the day
+DESK = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), "Desktop", "E1-DESK")
+EXPORTS = os.path.join(DESK, "exports")
+DISK_WARN_FLAG = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", ".disk_warned")
+
+
+def account_name(engine, book):
+    return "%s-%s" % (str(engine).upper(), str(book).upper())
+
+
+def book_fire(engine, trade_id, stock, side, entry, stop, family="", *, ts="", event=True):
+    """Book one fire into BOTH of an engine's books (FLAT at ₹1,000, ADAPTIVE at its shadow weight — cold-start
+    flat). Idempotent per (book, trade_id). Returns {book: result}. This is the only entry point an engine's
+    rule calls; the daytime loop supplies real fires (E21/E9/E3/YT on prices, E7/E12/E14/E4 on the stream)."""
+    out = {}
+    for book in BOOKS:
+        out[book] = EB.record_entry(account_name(engine, book), trade_id, stock, side, entry, stop,
+                                    family=family, ts=ts, event=event)
+    return out
+
+
+def close_fire(engine, trade_id, result_R, cause="", *, ts="", event=True):
+    """Close a fire in both of an engine's books. Returns {book: row}."""
+    return {book: EB.record_exit(account_name(engine, book), trade_id, result_R, cause=cause, ts=ts, event=event)
+            for book in BOOKS}
+
+
+# ---------- isolation guard ----------
+
+def _diary_only():
+    try:
+        return set(json.load(open(DIARY_ONLY, encoding="utf-8")).get("engines", []))
+    except Exception:
+        return set()
+
+
+def set_diary_only(engine, reason=""):
+    d = _diary_only()
+    d.add(str(engine).upper())
+    try:
+        rec = {"engines": sorted(d), "reasons": {}}
+        try:
+            rec["reasons"] = json.load(open(DIARY_ONLY, encoding="utf-8")).get("reasons", {})
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        rec["reasons"][str(engine).upper()] = reason
+        json.dump(rec, open(DIARY_ONLY, "w", encoding="utf-8"), indent=1)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+def is_diary_only(engine):
+    return str(engine).upper() in _diary_only()
+
+
+def guarded(engine, fn, *args, **kwargs):
+    """Run one engine's work in isolation. If it raises, log it, set that engine DIARY-ONLY for the day, and
+    return None — the caller keeps going for every other engine. A failing engine can never stop E1."""
+    try:
+        return fn(*args, **kwargs)
+    except Exception as e:
+        set_diary_only(engine, "%s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, str(e)[:120]))
+        _log("engine %s failed and was set diary-only: %s" % (engine, type(e).__name__))
+        return None
+
+
+def _log(msg):
+    try:
+        p = os.path.join(_REPO, "reports", "engine_isolation.log")
+        with open(p, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+            from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
+            ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))).isoformat()
+            f.write("%s %s\n" % (ts, msg))
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+# ---------- auto-export + disk warning ----------
+
+def _stream_files(day):
+    """Every recorded stream file dated `day` under the deriv store (depth, spreads, session starts, and the
+    day's own dirs). Read-only; we only ever ADD to a zip, never delete a source."""
+    store = os.path.join(DESK, "stores", "e1_deriv_store")
+    files = []
+    for pat in ("depth5/%s/*" % day, "depth5/%s.*" % day, "*/%s/*" % day):
+        files += glob.glob(os.path.join(store, pat))
+    # the day's manifests/spreads that carry the date in their rows are copied whole (small, append-only)
+    for nm in ("depth5/SPREADS.csv", "depth5/MANIFEST.csv", "depth5/SESSION-STARTS.csv"):
+        p = os.path.join(store, nm)
+        if os.path.exists(p):
+            files.append(p)
+    return sorted({f for f in files if os.path.isfile(f)})
+
+
+def export_day(day=None):
+    """Zip every stream's files for `day` into E1-DESK/exports/<day>.zip. Adds to the zip; NEVER deletes a
+    recorded source. Returns the zip path (or None if nothing to export). Idempotent: rewrites the day's zip."""
+    day = day or date.today().isoformat()
+    files = _stream_files(day)
+    if not files:
+        return None
+    os.makedirs(EXPORTS, exist_ok=True)
+    out = os.path.join(EXPORTS, "%s.zip" % day)
+    store = os.path.join(DESK, "stores", "e1_deriv_store")
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(out, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
+        for f in files:
+            try:
+                z.write(f, os.path.relpath(f, store))
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+    check_disk()
+    return out
+
+
+def _ten_day_bytes():
+    """~ten days of recording = 10 × the most recent day's recorded size (measured, not assumed). Falls back
+    to a small floor if nothing is recorded yet."""
+    store = os.path.join(DESK, "stores", "e1_deriv_store", "depth5")
+    days = sorted(d for d in glob.glob(os.path.join(store, "*")) if os.path.isdir(d))
+    if not days:
+        return 100 * 1024 * 1024
+    last = days[-1]
+    size = sum(os.path.getsize(os.path.join(r, f)) for r, _, fs in os.walk(last) for f in fs)
+    return max(size, 1) * 10
+
+
+def check_disk(notifier=None):
+    """One Telegram warning if free disk drops below ~ten days of recording. Warned-once via a flag."""
+    try:
+        import shutil
+        free = shutil.disk_usage(DESK).free
+    except Exception:
+        return None
+    need = _ten_day_bytes()
+    if free >= need:
+        try:
+            if os.path.exists(DISK_WARN_FLAG):
+                os.remove(DISK_WARN_FLAG)      # recovered → re-arm the warning
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        return {"free": free, "need": need, "warned": False}
+    if os.path.exists(DISK_WARN_FLAG):
+        return {"free": free, "need": need, "warned": True}    # already warned
+    open(DISK_WARN_FLAG, "w").close()
+    msg = ("Free disk is low: %.1f GB left, under ~ten days of recording (%.1f GB). Recorded data is never "
+           "deleted — please free some space." % (free / 1e9, need / 1e9))
+    (notifier or _notify)(msg)
+    return {"free": free, "need": need, "warned": True}
+
+
+def _notify(msg):
+    try:
+        from scripts import notify
+        notify.notify(msg)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+
+def _demo():
+    import tempfile
+    global DIARY_ONLY
+    saved = DIARY_ONLY
+    DIARY_ONLY = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "diaryonly_%d.json" % os.getpid())
+    try:
+        assert len(ENGINES) == 8 and set(BOOKS) == {"FLAT", "ADAPTIVE"}
+        assert account_name("E21", "flat") == "E21-FLAT"
+        # ISOLATION: a failing engine is set diary-only and returns None; a good one still runs
+        assert guarded("E9", lambda: 1 / 0) is None and is_diary_only("E9")
+        assert guarded("E21", lambda: "ok") == "ok" and not is_diary_only("E21")
+        # the disk check with an injected notifier: plenty of space -> no warning
+        note = []
+        r = check_disk(notifier=lambda m: note.append(m))
+        assert r is None or r.get("warned") in (True, False)
+        print("engine_accounts demo OK: 8 engines x 2 books, isolation guard (E9 fails alone, E21 fine), "
+              "disk check runs.")
+    finally:
+        DIARY_ONLY = saved
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    if "--demo" in sys.argv:
+        _demo()
+    elif "--export" in sys.argv:
+        print("exported:", export_day())
+    else:
+        print("engines:", ENGINES, "| accounts:", ACC.NEW_ENGINE_ACCOUNTS)

+def rehearse_new_engines(write=True):
+    """R-ALL-ENGINES-TOMORROW item 2: one rehearsal per fixed-rule engine — a synthetic signal fills BOTH books
+    (FLAT + ADAPTIVE) on TEMP ledgers and a card is written per book to the log. An engine whose rehearsal
+    RAISES or fails to fill is set DIARY-ONLY for the day (isolation: one bad engine never blocks the others,
+    and never blocks E1). Returns {engine: {"ok": bool, "reason": str}}."""
+    from scripts import engine_accounts as EA
+    from scripts import portfolio_card as PC
+    from engine1_rebuild import engine_book as EB, paper_account as ACC
+    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
+    lines = ["", "=== NEW-ENGINE REHEARSALS (temp ledgers; production books untouched) ==="]
+    results = {}
+    for eng in EA.ENGINES:
+        try:
+            # point BOTH of this engine's book ledgers at temp files so nothing real is booked
+            for book in EA.BOOKS:
+                ACC.ENGINE_LEDGERS[EA.account_name(eng, book)] = os.path.join(tmp, "%s-%s.csv" % (eng, book))
+            tid = "REH-%s-%s" % (eng, datetime.now(IST).strftime("%H%M%S"))
+            fills = EA.book_fire(eng, tid, "NIFTY", "buy", 100.0, 98.0, family="rsi", event=False)
+            ok = all(fills[b] and fills[b].get("risk_rupees", 0) > 0 for b in EA.BOOKS)
+            for book in EA.BOOKS:
+                risk = (fills[book] or {}).get("risk_rupees", 0)
+                card = PC.fire_card("[%s-%s]" % (eng, book), "NIFTY", "direct",
+                                    "one rehearsal signal", risk, "PAPER ORDER PLACED %s" % PC.R(risk))
+                lines.append("   CARD -> log: " + card.replace("\n", " "))
+            if ok:
+                results[eng] = {"ok": True, "reason": "one signal filled both books"}
+                lines.append("   %s: PASS" % eng)
+            else:
+                EA.set_diary_only(eng, "rehearsal did not fill both books")
+                results[eng] = {"ok": False, "reason": "did not fill both books"}
+                lines.append("   %s: FAIL -> diary only" % eng)
+        except Exception as e:
+            EA.set_diary_only(eng, "%s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, str(e)[:100]))
+            results[eng] = {"ok": False, "reason": "%s: %s" % (type(e).__name__, str(e)[:80])}
+            lines.append("   %s: FAIL (%s) -> diary only" % (eng, type(e).__name__))
+    if write:
+        try:
+            with open(LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+                f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    return results
+
+
 if __name__ == "__main__":
     ok = run()
+    reh = rehearse_new_engines()
     print("REHEARSAL", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL", "-> reports/REHEARSAL-DAY1.log")
+    print("new engines:", {e: r["ok"] for e, r in reh.items()})
     sys.exit(0 if ok else 1)

+"""R-ALL-ENGINES-TOMORROW: 8 engines x 2 books wired, the isolation guard, and per-engine rehearsals."""
+import scripts.engine_accounts as EA
+from engine1_rebuild import paper_account as ACC
+
+
+def test_sixteen_accounts_at_two_lakh():
+    assert len(ACC.NEW_ENGINE_ACCOUNTS) == 16
+    for name in ACC.NEW_ENGINE_ACCOUNTS:
+        a = ACC.account(name)
+        assert a["start_equity"] == ACC.ENGINE_START == 200000.0
+
+
+def test_book_fire_fills_both_books(tmp_path):
+    for book in EA.BOOKS:
+        ACC.ENGINE_LEDGERS[EA.account_name("E9", book)] = str(tmp_path / ("E9-%s.csv" % book))
+    out = EA.book_fire("E9", "t-iso-1", "NIFTY", "buy", 100.0, 98.0, family="rsi", event=False)
+    assert out["FLAT"]["risk_rupees"] == 1000.0            # flat = ₹1,000 per trade
+    assert out["ADAPTIVE"]["risk_rupees"] > 0              # adaptive cold-starts flat
+
+
+def test_isolation_a_failing_engine_cannot_stop_e1(tmp_path):
+    EA.DIARY_ONLY = str(tmp_path / "diary_only.json")
+    ran = {"e1": False}
+
+    def failing_engine():
+        raise RuntimeError("boom")
+
+    def e1_engine():
+        ran["e1"] = True
+        return "E1 traded"
+
+    # the loop runs each engine guarded; the failing one is isolated, E1 still runs after it
+    EA.guarded("E9", failing_engine)
+    result = EA.guarded("E1", e1_engine)
+    assert EA.is_diary_only("E9")                          # the bad engine is quarantined
+    assert not EA.is_diary_only("E1")                      # E1 is untouched
+    assert ran["e1"] and result == "E1 traded"            # E1 still traded
+
+
+def test_rehearsals_all_pass():
+    from scripts import rehearsal_day1 as RD
+    reh = RD.rehearse_new_engines(write=False)
+    assert len(reh) == 8
+    assert all(r["ok"] for r in reh.values()), reh
+
+
+def test_demo():
+    EA._demo()
